data-component="api-operation" id="server.info"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/info</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Get server info</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Return the server identity, connection URLs, paths, and readiness status.</p><!--!$--><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ServerInfo">ServerInfo</a></td><td>ServerInfo</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>server.info</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="server.pair"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/pair</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Create pairing code</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Create a short-lived, single-use code for a /auth/connect/:code pairing link.</p><!--!$--><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-PairingCode">PairingCode</a></td><td>PairingCode</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>server.pair</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="server.connect"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/auth/connect/{code}</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Redeem pairing code</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Redeem a pairing code. Browsers receive a session cookie and a redirect to the web app; requests that accept JSON receive a session token to use as the password.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>code</code></td><td>path</td><td><span data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/location</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Get location</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Resolve the requested location or the server default location.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Location.PublicInfo">Location.PublicInfo</a></td><td>Location.PublicInfo</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>location.get</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="location.reload"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/location/reload</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Reload configuration</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Shut down and rebuild every loaded location. Pending permissions and forms are cancelled; running sessions continue with fresh services at the next step boundary. Emits location.shutdown for client recovery and responds once all replacement builds settle.</p><!--!$--><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No Content></td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>503</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded">ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ServiceUnavailableError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>location.reload</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-agent"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-agent" title="Copy link to this section">Agent</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><details data-component="api-operation" id="agent.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/agent</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List agents</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Retrieve currently registered agents.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>agent.list</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="agent.get"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/agent/{agentID}</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Get agent</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Retrieve a single currently registered agent.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>agentID</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Agent.ID">Agent.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-AgentNotFoundErrorEncoded">AgentNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>AgentNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>agent.get</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-plugin"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-plugin" title="Copy link to this section">Plugin</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><p>Experimental plugin routes.</p><details data-component="api-operation" id="plugin.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/plugin</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List plugins</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Retrieve enabled server plugins and their current status.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>plugin.list</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="plugin.gui"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/plugin/gui</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Read plugin GUI bundle</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Read an active plugin's prebuilt CommonJS `./gui-experimental` bundle. Desktop app windows run its default export; the desktop app's main process runs its `main` export. Fails with not found when no active plugin has the id or the plugin exports no `./gui-experimental`.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>id</code></td><td>query</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Plugin.ID">Plugin.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-PluginNotFoundErrorEncoded">PluginNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>PluginNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>plugin.gui</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="plugin.check"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/plugin/check</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Check plugin updates</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Check one or all package plugins for available updates.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>plugin.check</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="plugin.update"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/plugin/update</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Update plugins</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Update package plugins concurrently and notify active locations to reload them. Responds once every update has finished; fails when any update fails.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No Content></td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>503</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded">ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ServiceUnavailableError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>plugin.update</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-session"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-session" title="Copy link to this section">Session</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><p>Experimental session routes.</p><details data-component="api-operation" id="session.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/session</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List sessions</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Retrieve sessions in the requested order. Items keep that order across pages; use cursor.next or cursor.previous to move through the ordered list.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>limit</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Maximum number of sessions to return. Defaults to the newest 50 sessions.</td></tr><tr><td><code>order</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><span data-slot="ref-type">"asc" | "desc"</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Session order for the first page. Use desc for newest first or asc for oldest first.</td></tr><tr><td><code>search</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Only sessions whose title contains this text, ignoring case. % and _ act as wildcards.</td></tr><tr><td><code>parentID</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Session.ID">Session.ID</a> | <span data-slot="ref-type">"null"</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Filter by parent session. Use null to return only root sessions.</td></tr><tr><td><code>archived</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">"true" | "false"</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Filter by archive state. Use true to return only archived sessions or false to exclude them. Omit to include both.</td></tr><tr><td><code>directory</code></td><td>query</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-AbsolutePath">AbsolutePath</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Only sessions whose directory is exactly this absolute path.</td></tr><tr><td><code>project</code></td><td>query</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Project.ID">Project.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>subpath</code></td><td>query</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-RelativePath">RelativePath</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>With project, only sessions at this path relative to the project directory.</td></tr><tr><td><code>cursor</code></td><td>query</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionsCursor">SessionsCursor</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Opaque cursor from a previous page's cursor.next or cursor.previous. It carries that page's filters and order, so other filters are ignored.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionsResponse">SessionsResponse</a></td><td>SessionsResponse</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidCursorErrorEncoded">InvalidCursorErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>InvalidCursorError | InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>session.list</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="session.create"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/session</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Create session</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Create a session at the requested location. A parentID creates a linked child session at its parent's location.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded">SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>SessionNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>session.create</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.session.stats"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/session/stats</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Get session statistics</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Aggregate local session activity, usage, and tool reliability for a time range.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>from</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Start of the range in Unix milliseconds, inclusive. Defaults to the earliest recorded message.</td></tr><tr><td><code>to</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>End of the range in Unix milliseconds, exclusive. Defaults to now.</td></tr><tr><td><code>project</code></td><td>query</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Project.ID">Project.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>timezone</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>IANA time zone used to group activity by day. Defaults to UTC.</td></tr><tr><td><code>tools</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">"none" | "summary" | "detail"</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>How much tool usage to report: none, summary totals, or per-tool detail. Defaults to summary.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.session.stats</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.session.import"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/session/import</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Import session</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Import a projected session transcript at the requested location. Accepts transcripts exported by earlier releases. If parentID is supplied, the parent session must already exist; import parents before children.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded">SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>SessionNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>409</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ConflictErrorEncoded">ConflictErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ConflictError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.session.import</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.session.export"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/session/{sessionID}/export</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Export session</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Export a complete projected session transcript.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>sessionID</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Session.ID">Session.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>sanitize</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">"true" | "false"</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Set to true to redact titles, message text, file data, paths and metadata from the export.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div <code>session.context</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="session.diff"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/session/{sessionID}/diff</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Diff session turns</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Structured per-file diffs of the files a turn changed. A turn runs from the first prompt after the session was last idle until its next idle marker, so prompts steered in while it was busy belong to the same turn; `to` extends the range through a later turn. Compares the range's first recorded snapshot with its last; a step still running in the active session compares against the working copy. Ranges that span a location change are rejected. In sessions without any idle marker, a prompt's turn spans until the next user message.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>sessionID</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Session.ID">Session.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>from</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Session.Message.ID">Session.Message.ID</a></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>User message whose turn to diff. Defaults to the turn of the newest user message.</td></tr><tr><td><code>to</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Session.Message.ID">Session.Message.ID</a></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Later user message whose turn ends the range. Defaults to the turn of `from` alone.</td></tr><tr><td><code>context</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Unchanged lines around each hunk. Omit for full-file patches.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-MessageNotFoundErrorEncoded">MessageNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded">SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>MessageNotFoundError | SessionNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>500</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnknownErrorEncoded">UnknownErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnknownError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>session.diff</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="session.inbox.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/session/{sessionID}/inbox</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List session inbox</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>List user, synthetic, compaction and move items not yet delivered to the agent, oldest first. data-slot="api-body"><p>Experimental durable session event log. Reads events after an exclusive aggregate sequence and continues with live events when follow=true.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>sessionID</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>after</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Only return events after this sequence number.</td></tr><tr><td><code>follow</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">"true" | "false"</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Set to true to keep the stream open and send live events after the stored ones.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded">SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>SessionNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/session/{sessionID}/interrupt</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Interrupt session execution</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Stop the session's agent if it is running in this server process; interrupted=false means nothing was running. With resume=true it continues with pending steer items, compaction and moves, while queued messages keep waiting.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>sessionID</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Set to true to resume pending steering input and next-in-line control items after interrupting.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionInterruptResponse">SessionInterruptResponse</a></td><td>SessionInterruptResponse</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded">SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>SessionNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID href="#schema-FormAlreadySettledErrorEncoded">FormAlreadySettledErrorEncoded</a></td><td>FormAlreadySettledError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>session.form.reply</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="session.environment"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="put">put</span><code>/api/session/{sessionID}/environment</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Set session environment</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Replace the process environment used by local shell commands for this session.</p><div viewer as viewed.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No <code>session.view</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="session.message.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/session/{sessionID}/message</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Get session messages</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>List a session's messages, optionally filtered by type. Follow cursor.next or cursor.previous with the same type filter. Items still in the inbox aren't included.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>sessionID</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Session.ID">Session.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>limit</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Maximum number of messages to return. When omitted, the endpoint returns its default page size.</td></tr><tr><td><code>order</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><span data-slot="ref-type">"asc" | "desc"</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Message order for the first page. Use desc for newest first or asc for oldest first.</td></tr><tr><td><code>cursor</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Opaque pagination cursor returned as cursor.previous or cursor.next in the previous response. Do not combine with order.</td></tr><tr><td><code>type</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><span data-slot="ref-type">"agent-switched" | "model-switched" | "location-switched" | "user" | "synthetic" | "system" | "skill" | "shell" | "assistant" | "compaction" | "idle"</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Filter by message type before pagination. When omitted, all message types are returned. Pass the same type when following cursors.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionMessagesResponse">SessionMessagesResponse</a></td><td>SessionMessagesResponse</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidCursorErrorEncoded">InvalidCursorErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>InvalidCursorError | InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded">SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>SessionNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>500</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnknownErrorEncoded">UnknownErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnknownError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>session.message.list</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-model"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-model" title="Copy link to this section">Model</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><p>Experimental model routes.</p><details data-component="api-operation" id="model.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/model</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List models</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Retrieve the current snapshot of available models of the requested types, chat by default, ordered by release date within each type. The snapshot may precede initial plugin settlement.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>types</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Model.Type">Model.Type</a> | <span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Model.Type">Model.Type</a>[]</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Only list models of these types. Defaults to chat, the only type offered for sessions.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>503</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded">ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ServiceUnavailableError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>model.list</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="model.default"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/model/default</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Get default model</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Retrieve the model used when a session has no explicit model selection.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>503</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded">ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ServiceUnavailableError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>model.default</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-generate"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-generate" title="Copy link to this section">Generate</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><p>Experimental one-shot generation routes.</p><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.generate.text"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/generate/text</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Generate text</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Run one stateless model generation using the server's base configuration and return the assistant text. data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/generate/image</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Generate images</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Generate or edit images with an image model using the server's base configuration and return them as base64. body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-GenerateImageResponse">GenerateImageResponse</a></td><td>GenerateImageResponse</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" <code>experimental.generate.image</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.generate.video.create"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/generate/video</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Create a video generation</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Submit a video job to a video model using the server's base configuration and return its ID once the provider accepted it. The server polls the job and stores the finished videos as blobs; read the job with GET /api/experimental/generate/video/:id and the video with GET /api/experimental/blob/:id. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Generate.VideoRequest">Generate.VideoRequest</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-GenerateVideoCreateResponse">GenerateVideoCreateResponse</a></td><td>GenerateVideoCreateResponse</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ProviderNotFoundErrorEncoded">ProviderNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ModelNotFoundErrorEncoded">ModelNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>ProviderNotFoundError | ModelNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>422</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnsupportedModelErrorEncoded">UnsupportedModelErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnsupportedModelError</td></tr><tr><td><code>502</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ProviderRequestErrorEncoded">ProviderRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ProviderRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>503</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded">ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ServiceUnavailableError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.generate.video.create</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.generate.video.get"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/generate/video/{id}</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Read a video generation</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Read a video job: running, completed with its videos' blobs, or error with a reason. Jobs survive a server restart, which resumes polling running ones. A job is not found 24 hours after it finished, or once none of its blobs remain. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>id</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Generate.VideoID">Generate.VideoID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-GenerateVideoGetResponse">GenerateVideoGetResponse</a></td><td>GenerateVideoGetResponse</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-VideoNotFoundErrorEncoded">VideoNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>VideoNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.generate.video.get</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.generate.decisions"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/generate/decisions</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Generate decisions</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Ask a decision model typed predicate, choice and score questions about the input and return one probability-bearing answer per question, in question order. Stateless: nothing is stored.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Generate.DecisionRequest">Generate.DecisionRequest</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>name</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td>Name of the MCP server the client hosts.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ForbiddenErrorEncoded">ForbiddenErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ForbiddenError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.mcp.client.token</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.mcp.client"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/client-mcp</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Attach client MCP server</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Upgrade to a WebSocket over which the client hosts an MCP server named after `name`; its tools stay available while the socket is open. One JSON-RPC message per text frame. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>name</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td>Name of the MCP server the client hosts.</td></tr><tr><td><code>ticket</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Single-use ticket from experimental.mcp.client.token, which authorizes the connection without the usual credentials.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">boolean</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ForbiddenErrorEncoded">ForbiddenErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ForbiddenError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.mcp.client</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-credential"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-credential" title="Copy link to data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" id="tag-filesystem"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-filesystem" title="Copy link to this section">Filesystem</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><p>Experimental location-scoped filesystem routes.</p><details data-component="api-operation" id="fs.read"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/fs/read/*</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Read file</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Stream one file relative to the requested location with ETag, Last-Modified, and single-range HTTP Range support (206 Partial Content, 416 Range Not Satisfiable, and 304 Not Modified; multi-range and malformed Range headers fall back to 200).</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string&lt;binary></span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-FileNotFoundErrorEncoded">FileNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>FileNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>fs.read</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="fs.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/fs/list</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List directory</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>List direct children using an absolute path or a path relative to the requested location, including parents and siblings outside its directory. Entry paths remain relative to the requested location; listing does not switch locations.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>path</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>An absolute path or a path relative to the requested location. Defaults to the location directory.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-FileNotFoundErrorEncoded">FileNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>FileNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>fs.list</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="fs.find"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/fs/find</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Find files</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Find recursively ranked filesystem entries relative to the requested location.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>query</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>type</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">"file" | "directory"</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>limit</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>fs.find</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.fs.write"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/fs/write</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Write file</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Write the raw request body to an absolute path or a path relative to the requested location, creating parent directories, and return the resolved absolute path. Unlike read, the target is not confined to the location. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>path</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td>An absolute path or a path relative to the requested location. Missing parent directories are created.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/octet-stream</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string&lt;binary></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.fs.write</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.fs.watch"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/fs/watch</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Watch file</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Watch one file relative to the requested location for 24 hours, publishing filesystem.changed on that location when it changes. Watching an already watched file resets its expiry. Paths follow read confinement. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No Content></td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-FileNotFoundErrorEncoded">FileNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>FileNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>503</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded">ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ServiceUnavailableError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.fs.watch</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.fs.unwatch"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="delete">delete</span><code>/api/experimental/fs/watch</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Unwatch file</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Stop watching one file relative to the requested location. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No Content></td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.fs.unwatch</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-command"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-command" title="Copy link to this section">Command</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><p>Experimental command routes.</p><details data-component="api-operation" id="command.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/command</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List commands</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Retrieve currently registered commands.</p><div href="#schema-ForbiddenErrorEncoded">ForbiddenErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ForbiddenError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-PtyNotFoundErrorEncoded">PtyNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>PtyNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>pty.connect.token</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="pty.connect"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/pty/{ptyID}/connect</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Connect to PTY session</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Establish a WebSocket connection streaming PTY output and accepting terminal input.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>ptyID</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Pty.ID">Pty.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>location[directory]</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Absolute path of the location directory. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>cursor</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Output offset to replay from. Use -1 to start at the current end; omit to replay all retained output.</td></tr><tr><td><code>ticket</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Single-use ticket from pty.connect.token, which authorizes the connection without the usual credentials.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">boolean</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ForbiddenErrorEncoded">ForbiddenErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ForbiddenError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-PtyNotFoundErrorEncoded">PtyNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>PtyNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>pty.connect</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-persistentpty"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-persistentpty" title="Copy link to this section">Persistent PTY</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><p>Prototype persistent PTY routes.</p><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.persistentPty.read"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/session/{sessionID}/terminal/read</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Read the session's most recently controlled terminal</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Read the last physical rows without changing selection or taking control. Omitted lines uses the live terminal height; larger counts include retained history. Blank rows are preserved. Screen dimensions and cursor remain relative to the live screen. Returns null when no current terminal exists. Selection is server-local and resets on restart. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>sessionID</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Session.ID">Session.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>lines</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Number of trailing rows to read, up to 65535. Defaults to the live terminal height; larger counts include retained history.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>503</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded">ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ServiceUnavailableError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.persistentPty.read</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.persistentPty.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>ptyID</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>cursor</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Output offset to replay from. Defaults to 0.</td></tr><tr><td><code>role</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Attach as controller (the default) or observer.</td></tr><tr><td><code>takeover</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Set to true to take over control of the terminal.</td></tr><tr><td><code>input_protocol</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Set to 1 to send framed input messages instead of raw terminal input.</td></tr><tr><td><code>ticket</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Single-use ticket from experimental.persistentPty.connectToken, which authorizes the connection without the usual credentials.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div
