title="Copy link to this section">Experimental</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.media.get"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/media/{hash}</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Read media blob</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Read a content-addressed media blob by SHA-256 hash with immutable Cache-Control, ETag, and 304 Not Modified support. Only blobs a stored message or inbox item still references are served. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>hash</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-MediaHash">MediaHash</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string&lt;binary></span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-FileNotFoundErrorEncoded">FileNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>FileNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.media.get</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.blob.get"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/blob/{id}</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Read a blob</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Stream a stored blob's bytes, such as a generated video, with single byte Range, ETag and 304 Not Modified support. A blob is deleted a week after it was stored. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>id</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Blob.ID">Blob.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string&lt;binary></span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-BlobNotFoundErrorEncoded">BlobNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>BlobNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.blob.get</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.automation.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/automation</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List automations</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>List automations, oldest first. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees. Set automations to false in the directory's config to disable access.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>sessionID</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Session.ID">Session.ID</a></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Only automations created by this Session.</td></tr><tr><td><code>directory</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Only automations in the project that owns this directory.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" <code>experimental.automation.list</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.automation.create"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/automation</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Create automation</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Create an automation that sends a prompt, runs a Code Mode program, or runs a shell command at a time, every interval, or on a cron. Creating an MCP event watch requires the automation_create tool in a local session; a session ID over HTTP is not a watch authorization. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees. Set automations to false in the directory's config to disable access.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.CreatePayload">Automation.CreatePayload</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ForbiddenErrorEncoded">ForbiddenErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ForbiddenError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded">SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>SessionNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.automation.create</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.automation.get"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/automation/{automationID}</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Get automation</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Read one automation. Set automations to false in the directory's config to disable access.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>automationID</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.ID">Automation.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ForbiddenErrorEncoded">ForbiddenErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ForbiddenError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-AutomationNotFoundErrorEncoded">AutomationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>AutomationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.automation.pause</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.automation.resume"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/automation/{automationID}/resume</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Resume automation</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Resume a paused automation. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>sessionID</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Session.ID">Session.ID</a></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Filter to background jobs owned by this session.</td></tr><tr><td><code>kind</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Job.Kind">Job.Kind</a></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Filter by background job kind.</td></tr><tr><td><code>status</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Job.Status">Job.Status</a></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Filter by job status (running, completed, error, or cancelled).</td></tr><tr><td><code>descendants</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><span data-slot="ref-type">"true" | "false"</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>When sessionID is set, also include background jobs owned by child and nested subagent sessions.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded">SessionNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>SessionNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.job.list</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.job.cancel"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/job/{id}/cancel</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Cancel background job</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Cancel a running background job, interrupting the work it started. Cancelling a job that already finished does nothing. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>id</code></td><td>path</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No Content></td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.job.cancel</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.machine.link"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/machine/link</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Connect a machine</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Upgrade to a WebSocket that carries a machine's link to this server: binary messages only, each at most 65,535 bytes. It takes no server password. The link's own handshake, which starts with the machine's first message, is the only way in: it admits a key this server has paired or a valid join string, and closes the socket on anything else without saying why. Behind the machines flag; without it the route does not exist. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><!--!$--><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">boolean</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.machine.link</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.machine.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/experimental/machine</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List machines</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>List known machines and whether each is paired and online. Behind the machines flag. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><!--!$--><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ForbiddenErrorEncoded">ForbiddenErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ForbiddenError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.machine.list</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.machine.add"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/experimental/machine</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Add or invite a machine</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Add a machine by name if it does not exist, then create a single-use join string carrying the given reach addresses. Behind the machines flag. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Machine.AddPayload">Machine.AddPayload</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ForbiddenErrorEncoded">ForbiddenErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ForbiddenError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-MachineNotFoundErrorEncoded">MachineNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>MachineNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>409</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ConflictErrorEncoded">ConflictErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ConflictError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.machine.add</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="experimental.machine.remove"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="delete">delete</span><code>/api/experimental/machine/{name}</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Remove a machine</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Forget a machine by name, closing any live link and revoking its key. Behind the machines flag. Experimental: may change without compatibility guarantees.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>name</code></td><td>path</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No Content></td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>403</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ForbiddenErrorEncoded">ForbiddenErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ForbiddenError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-MachineNotFoundErrorEncoded">MachineNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>MachineNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>experimental.machine.remove</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-shell"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-shell" title="Copy link to this section">Shell</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><p>Location-scoped shell command routes.</p><details data-component="api-operation" id="shell.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/shell</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List running shell commands</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>List currently running shell commands for a location. Exited commands are not included.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Combined stdout/stderr is captured to a file pageable via output.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" <code>shell.create</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="shell.get"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/shell/{id}</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Get shell command</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Get one shell command, including its status and exit code once exited.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>id</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ShellNotFoundErrorEncoded">ShellNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>ShellNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>shell.get</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="shell.remove"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="delete">delete</span><code>/api/shell/{id}</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Remove shell command</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Terminate and remove one shell command and its retained output.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>id</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Shell.ID">Shell.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No Content></td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>shell.remove</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="shell.output"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/shell/{id}/output</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Read shell output</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Page through captured combined output by absolute byte cursor.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>id</code></td><td>path</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Shell.ID">Shell.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>cursor</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Byte offset to read from, such as the cursor of the previous page. Defaults to 0.</td></tr><tr><td><code>limit</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Maximum number of bytes to return. Defaults to 65536.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ShellNotFoundErrorEncoded">ShellNotFoundErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>ShellNotFoundError | LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>shell.output</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-reference"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span section">Worktree</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><p>Project-based worktree management routes.</p><details data-component="api-operation" id="worktree.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/worktree</code><span data-slot="api-summary">List worktrees</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Return the project's saved worktree inventory without loading configuration or running discovery.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>projectID</code></td><td>query</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Project.ID">Project.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Worktree.List">Worktree.List</a></td><td>Worktree.List</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ProjectNotFoundErrorEncoded">ProjectNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ProjectNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>worktree.list</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="worktree.create"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/worktree</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Create worktree</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Load the project's canonical configuration, create a local worktree using its selected strategy, then run the project's setup script.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Worktree.CreateInput">Worktree.CreateInput</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Worktree.Info">Worktree.Info</a></td><td>Worktree.Info</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-WorktreeErrorEncoded">WorktreeErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>WorktreeError | InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ProjectNotFoundErrorEncoded">ProjectNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ProjectNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>worktree.create</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="worktree.remove"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="delete">delete</span><code>/api/worktree</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Remove worktree</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Load the project's canonical configuration and remove a saved worktree using its recorded strategy.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Worktree.RemoveInput">Worktree.RemoveInput</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No Content></td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-WorktreeErrorEncoded">WorktreeErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>WorktreeError | InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ProjectNotFoundErrorEncoded">ProjectNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ProjectNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>worktree.remove</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="worktree.refresh"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/worktree/refresh</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Refresh worktrees</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Load the project's canonical configuration, discover worktrees across known checkout roots using all available strategies, and reconcile saved state.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Request body</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>application/json</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No Content></td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-WorktreeErrorEncoded">WorktreeErrorEncoded</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></span></td><td>WorktreeError | InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ProjectNotFoundErrorEncoded">ProjectNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ProjectNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>worktree.refresh</code></p></div></details></section><section><h2 id="tag-vcs"><button type="button" data-slot="heading-link" data-heading-id="tag-vcs" title="Copy link to this section">VCS</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><p>Location-scoped version control routes.</p><details data-component="api-operation" id="vcs.init"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/vcs/init</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Initialize VCS repository</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Initialize a repository using the selected VCS provider in a markerless project's directory and refresh its location services. Omitting provider defaults to git; built-in git and hg providers support initialization. An unknown provider returns 400; a registered provider without init returns 501.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>provider</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>VCS provider to initialize. Defaults to git.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>204</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">empty</span></td><td>&lt;No Content></td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>409</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ConflictErrorEncoded">ConflictErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ConflictError</td></tr><tr><td><code>501</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-VcsInitNotSupportedErrorEncoded">VcsInitNotSupportedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>VcsInitNotSupportedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>503</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded">ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ServiceUnavailableError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>vcs.init</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="vcs.get"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/vcs</code><span data-slot="api-summary">VCS info</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Get current and default branch information for the requested location.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>vcs.get</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="vcs.base"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/vcs/base</code><span data-slot="api-summary">VCS review base</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Infer a local review base from named branch creation history, or the repository default only when currently on that branch. Returns null before the first commit or when the provider lacks base metadata; ambiguous Git history requires an explicit base on diff requests.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr><tr><td><code>503</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded">ServiceUnavailableErrorEncoded</a></td><td>ServiceUnavailableError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>vcs.base</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="vcs.status"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/vcs/status</code><span data-slot="api-summary">VCS status</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>List uncommitted working-copy changes relative to the requested location.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>vcs.status</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="vcs.branch.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/vcs/branch</code><span data-slot="api-summary">VCS branches</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>List local and remote branches available at the requested location.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>search</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>limit</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>404</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded">LocationNotFoundErrorEncoded</a></td><td>LocationNotFoundError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>vcs.branch.list</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="vcs.diff"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="get">get</span><code>/api/vcs/diff</code><span data-slot="api-summary">VCS diff</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Diff HEAD to the working copy (working), the base merge-base to the working copy (branch), or the base merge-base to HEAD (committed). Omitting base preserves repository-default comparison; supplying it overrides the comparison without saving it.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>location</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Location to run the request in, sent as location[directory]. Omit to use the server's default location.</td></tr><tr><td><code>mode</code></td><td>query</td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Vcs.Mode">Vcs.Mode</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" section">Debug</button><span data-slot="heading-copied" aria-live="polite" data-pagefind-ignore></span></h2><details data-component="api-operation" id="debug.heapDump"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-method="post">post</span><code>/api/debug/heap-dump</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Write a heap snapshot</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>Write a JavaScript heap snapshot of the server process into its log directory, or the requested directory, and return the server-local path once the file is complete. Pauses the server while the heap serializes and can temporarily increase its memory use. The snapshot can contain credentials, prompts, and file contents.</p><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Parameter</th><th>In</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>directory</code></td><td>query</td><td><span data-slot="ref-type"><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Absolute server-local directory to write the snapshot into, created when missing. Defaults to the server's log directory.</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Type</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>200</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td>Success</td></tr><tr><td><code>400</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-InvalidRequestErrorEncoded">InvalidRequestErrorEncoded</a></td><td>InvalidRequestError</td></tr><tr><td><code>401</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnauthorizedErrorEncoded">UnauthorizedErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnauthorizedError</td></tr><tr><td><code>500</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-UnknownErrorEncoded">UnknownErrorEncoded</a></td><td>UnknownError</td></tr></tbody></table></div><p data-slot="operation-id">Operation ID <code>debug.heapDump</code></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="debug.location.list"><summary data-slot="api-head"><span data-component="badge" data-component="api-operation" id="schema-Automation.ActionInput"><summary data-slot="api-head"><code>Automation.ActionInput</code><span data-slot="api-summary">Automation.PromptActionInput | Automation.CodeActionInput | Automation.ShellActionInput</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>What it does: { type: "prompt", prompt, to? } sends a prompt; { type: "code", code, args? } runs Code Mode; { type: "shell", command } runs a fixed shell command.</p><p><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.PromptActionInput">Automation.PromptActionInput</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.CodeActionInput">Automation.CodeActionInput</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.ShellActionInput">Automation.ShellActionInput</a></span></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="schema-Automation.CodeActionInput"><summary data-slot="api-head"><code>Automation.CodeActionInput</code><span data-slot="api-summary">object</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Field</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>type</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">"code"</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>code</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td>Code Mode JavaScript run on each trigger with no model (32 KB output cap). Call tools statically as tools.&lt;ns>.&lt;tool>(...) or tools["ns-name"].tool(...); each is authorized once at creation to run unattended. A shell command still running at its timeout is stopped and the call throws; it never moves to the background. Receives input = { state, now, runs, args } (also globals): state starts null, now is epoch ms, runs counts earlier runs. Event triggers also receive input.event = { id, timestamp, data }; data follows the event type's payloadSchema. Return nothing, or { state?, done?, send? }. JSON state is kept for the next run; done: true stops. lastOutput shows the delivery target and any logs. Return at most one send per run. { send: { prompt } } steers the creating session; { send: { prompt, to: { subagent } } } starts a permitted child that does not report back. No session IDs. Omit send to stay quiet. A rejected send commits neither state nor done. Example code string: if (runs &lt; 2) return; return { done: true, send: { prompt: "Three checks elapsed. Review the build." } }</td></tr><tr><td><code>args</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">object</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="schema-Automation.CreatePayload"><summary data-slot="api-head"><code>Automation.CreatePayload</code><span data-slot="api-summary">object</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><div data-slot="api-table"><table><thead><tr><th>Field</th><th>Type</th><th>Required</th><th>Description</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>sessionID</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Session.ID">Session.ID</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>directory</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td>Where it runs when there is no Session. Defaults to the server user's home folder.</td></tr><tr><td><code>name</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td>A short label shown in lists.</td></tr><tr><td><code>trigger</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.Trigger">Automation.Trigger</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>action</code></td><td><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.ActionInput">Automation.ActionInput</a></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>expires</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="schema-Automation.Duration"><summary Automation.Trigger.Event</span></summary><div data-slot="api-body"><p>When it runs: { type: "at", at: "in 20 minutes" | "2026-10-27T09:00" }, { type: "every", every: "1 hour" } or { type: "cron", cron: "0 9 * * 1-5" }, each with an optional tz. Or { type: "event", server, event, args? } for a connected MCP server advertising push delivery.</p><p><span data-slot="ref-type"><a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.Trigger.At">Automation.Trigger.At</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.Trigger.Every">Automation.Trigger.Every</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.Trigger.Cron">Automation.Trigger.Cron</a> | <a data-slot="ref-type" href="#schema-Automation.Trigger.Event">Automation.Trigger.Event</a></span></p></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="schema-Automation.Trigger.At"><summary data-slot="api-head"><code>Automation.Trigger.At</code><span data-slot="api-summary">object</span></summary><div data-slot="ref-type">"at"</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td></td></tr><tr><td><code>at</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required" data-required="">Required</span></td><td>An instant with an offset, a wall-clock time in tz such as "2026-10-27T09:00", or "in 10 minutes".</td></tr><tr><td><code>tz</code></td><td><span data-slot="ref-type">string</span></td><td><span data-slot="ref-required">Optional</span></td><td></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></details><details data-component="api-operation" id="schema-Automation.Trigger.Cron"><summary
